### Step 8 of 21 · Describe ink depth and surface slack (`96zo-describe-ink-depth-and-surface-slack`)

- **Goal:** Type the current axis record and depth-carrying error budget.
- **Data Flow Trace:** `Follow gpu_geometry.rs, triangle.wgsl, gpu_mesh.rs, instance_gpu.rs and renderer.rs in this cumulative checkpoint; compare the maintained engine/gpu/ and shaders/triangle.wgsl ownership.`
- **Files You Touch:** `src/ink_plane.wgsl` (*Type the current axis and tolerance math once, with the declared forward-depth reader adapter.*) · `src/finite_visibility.wgsl` (*Keep surface fitting as a shared shader helper file.*) · `src/finite_sources.rs` (*Expand the helper before compiling either color or integer ink shaders.*)

A thick stroke covers pixels beside its center line.

Comparing its center depth with an unrelated side pixel can clip a coplanar stroke. Prepare the current InkAxis record: center, depth, direction, slope and sag. Sag represents a curved source edge rising above its display facets. Use the current relative rounding and subpixel slope tolerances rather than a fixed world-space bias. Keep one helper for carrying a surface depth to the stroke. For now, the depth reader explicitly converts the teaching forward-depth image; reversed camera depth will remove that conversion. These helpers do not change visibility yet.

> **Self-Check Question:** *Why does the tolerance include a pixel lever?*  
> **Answer:** Carrying a sampled plane farther amplifies depth error from subpixel rasterization. The error bound depends on slope and distance, not only the center depth.

- **Hands-On Lab Experiment:** Compile and compare the unchanged CAD view. Follow the units of each InkAxis field before connecting the helpers.

- **Goal:** Type the current fallback that carries a neighboring plane to the stroke axis.
- **Data Flow Trace:** `Follow gpu_geometry.rs, triangle.wgsl, gpu_mesh.rs, instance_gpu.rs and renderer.rs in this cumulative checkpoint; compare the maintained engine/gpu/ and shaders/triangle.wgsl ownership.`

A surface can end at the stroke, so first try a one-pixel step away from it and then the opposite direction.

Accept a neighbor pair only when the next slope agrees within the current kink bound. Express the center offset as a combination of stroke direction and the neighbor step; carry the sampled depth using the stroke and surface slopes. If neither side forms a plane, compare raw depth with the relative tolerance. These exact current helpers stay inactive until the primitive-gradient, point-disc and caller stages are ready.

> **Self-Check Question:** *Why inspect a second neighbor?*  
> **Answer:** Two pixels can cross a depth discontinuity. A third pixel tests whether the adjacent differences describe one surface.

- **Hands-On Lab Experiment:** Sketch a coplanar stroke and an occluder edge. Work through the two neighbor choices and the carried depth; the current pixels remain unchanged at this preparatory boundary.

Type the current neighbor validation, perpendicular step and axis-plane carry without duplicating the tolerance helpers.

<details>
<summary>Locate the existing block</summary>

```wgsl
fn ink_sag_slack(sag: vec2<f32>, gradient: vec2<f32>) -> f32 {
    return sag.x + sag.y * (abs(gradient.x) + abs(gradient.y));
}
fn ink_tolerance(depth: f32, slope: f32, lever: f32) -> f32 {
    return abs(depth) * DEPTH_REL_TOL + abs(slope) * SLOPE_PX * (1.0 + lever);
}
fn ink_carry_visible(predicted: f32, z: f32, depth: f32, tolerance: f32) -> bool {
    if (z > depth + abs(depth) * DEPTH_REL_TOL) {
        return abs(predicted - depth) <= tolerance;
    }
    return predicted <= depth + tolerance;
}
```

</details>

After that block, type:

```wgsl
fn ink_pair_planar(pixel: vec2<f32>, dir: vec2<f32>, z: f32, sample: u32) -> bool {
    let z_side = ink_depth(pixel + dir, sample);
    if (z_side == 0.0) {
        return false;
    }
    let z_far = ink_depth(pixel + dir * 2.0, sample);
    if (z_far == 0.0) {
        return true;
    }
    let g = z_side - z;
    let g_far = z_far - z_side;
    return abs(g_far - g) <= abs(z) * DEPTH_REL_TOL + KINK * (abs(g) + abs(g_far));
}
fn ink_step(pixel: vec2<f32>, axis: InkAxis) -> vec2<f32> {
    let perp = vec2<f32>(-axis.along.y, axis.along.x);
    var step = vec2<f32>(sign(perp.x), 0.0);
    if (abs(perp.y) > abs(perp.x)) {
        step = vec2<f32>(0.0, sign(perp.y));
    }
    return select(step, -step, dot(pixel - axis.at, step) < 0.0);
}
fn ink_axis_visible(pixel: vec2<f32>, axis: InkAxis, sample: u32) -> bool {
    let z = ink_depth(pixel, sample);
    if (z == 0.0) {
        return true;
    }
    let step = ink_step(pixel, axis);
    var side = step;
    if (!ink_pair_planar(pixel, side, z, sample)) {
        side = -step;
        if (!ink_pair_planar(pixel, side, z, sample)) {
            return z <= axis.depth + abs(axis.depth) * DEPTH_REL_TOL;
        }
    }
    let z_side = ink_depth(pixel + side, sample);
    let e = axis.at - pixel;
    let det = axis.along.x * side.y - axis.along.y * side.x;
    let a = (e.x * side.y - e.y * side.x) / det;
    let b = (axis.along.x * e.y - axis.along.y * e.x) / det;
    let g = z_side - z;
    let predicted = z + a * axis.slope + b * g;
    let tolerance = ink_tolerance(axis.depth, abs(g) + abs(axis.slope), abs(b)) + ink_sag_slack(axis.sag, vec2<f32>(g, axis.slope));
    return ink_carry_visible(predicted, z, axis.depth, tolerance);
}
```

```rust
use crate::pick_window::Window;

pub(crate) struct Image {
    pub texture: wgpu::Texture,
    pub colour: wgpu::TextureView,
    pub depth: wgpu::TextureView,
    pub section:wgpu::TextureView,
    pub readback: wgpu::Buffer,
    pub window: Window,
}

impl Image {
    pub fn new(device: &wgpu::Device, window: Window) -> Self {
        let size = wgpu::Extent3d { width: window.draw[2], height: window.draw[3], depth_or_array_layers: 1 };
        let texture = device.create_texture(&wgpu::TextureDescriptor { label: Some("bounded pick image"), size,
            mip_level_count: 1, sample_count: 1, dimension: wgpu::TextureDimension::D2,
            format: wgpu::TextureFormat::Rg32Uint, usage: wgpu::TextureUsages::RENDER_ATTACHMENT | wgpu::TextureUsages::COPY_SRC, view_formats: &[] });
        let colour = texture.create_view(&Default::default());
        let depth = device.create_texture(&wgpu::TextureDescriptor { label: Some("pick depth"), size,
            mip_level_count: 1, sample_count: 1, dimension: wgpu::TextureDimension::D2,
            format: crate::depth::FORMAT, usage: wgpu::TextureUsages::RENDER_ATTACHMENT|wgpu::TextureUsages::TEXTURE_BINDING, view_formats: &[] }).create_view(&Default::default());
        // GPU image rows need 256-byte copy alignment; the decoder skips that padding and reads only the logical columns.
        let readback = device.create_buffer(&wgpu::BufferDescriptor { label: Some("bounded pick readback"), size: window.bytes(),
            usage: wgpu::BufferUsages::COPY_DST | wgpu::BufferUsages::MAP_READ, mapped_at_creation: false });
        let section=device.create_texture(&wgpu::TextureDescriptor{label:Some("bounded section depth"),size,mip_level_count:1,sample_count:1,dimension:wgpu::TextureDimension::D2,format:wgpu::TextureFormat::Rg32Float,usage:wgpu::TextureUsages::RENDER_ATTACHMENT|wgpu::TextureUsages::TEXTURE_BINDING,view_formats:&[]}).create_view(&Default::default());
        Self { texture, colour, depth, section,readback, window }
    }

}
```

```rust
use crate::pick_window::Window;

pub(crate) struct Image {
    pub texture: wgpu::Texture,
    pub colour: wgpu::TextureView,
    pub depth: wgpu::TextureView,
    pub primitive:crate::primitive_image::Image,
    pub section:wgpu::TextureView,
    pub readback: wgpu::Buffer,
    pub window: Window,
}

impl Image {
    pub fn new(device: &wgpu::Device, window: Window) -> Self {
        let size = wgpu::Extent3d { width: window.draw[2], height: window.draw[3], depth_or_array_layers: 1 };
        let texture = device.create_texture(&wgpu::TextureDescriptor { label: Some("bounded pick image"), size,
            mip_level_count: 1, sample_count: 1, dimension: wgpu::TextureDimension::D2,
            format: wgpu::TextureFormat::Rg32Uint, usage: wgpu::TextureUsages::RENDER_ATTACHMENT | wgpu::TextureUsages::COPY_SRC, view_formats: &[] });
        let colour = texture.create_view(&Default::default());
        let depth = device.create_texture(&wgpu::TextureDescriptor { label: Some("pick depth"), size,
            mip_level_count: 1, sample_count: 1, dimension: wgpu::TextureDimension::D2,
            format: crate::depth::FORMAT, usage: wgpu::TextureUsages::RENDER_ATTACHMENT|wgpu::TextureUsages::TEXTURE_BINDING, view_formats: &[] }).create_view(&Default::default());
        // GPU image rows need 256-byte copy alignment; the decoder skips that padding and reads only the logical columns.
        let readback = device.create_buffer(&wgpu::BufferDescriptor { label: Some("bounded pick readback"), size: window.bytes(),
            usage: wgpu::BufferUsages::COPY_DST | wgpu::BufferUsages::MAP_READ, mapped_at_creation: false });
        let section=device.create_texture(&wgpu::TextureDescriptor{label:Some("bounded section depth"),size,mip_level_count:1,sample_count:1,dimension:wgpu::TextureDimension::D2,format:wgpu::TextureFormat::Rg32Float,usage:wgpu::TextureUsages::RENDER_ATTACHMENT|wgpu::TextureUsages::TEXTURE_BINDING,view_formats:&[]}).create_view(&Default::default());
        let primitive=crate::primitive_image::Image::new(device,[size.width,size.height]);
        Self { texture, colour, depth, primitive, section,readback, window }
    }

}
```

```rust
pub fn crop_clipping_layout(device:&wgpu::Device)->wgpu::BindGroupLayout{
    let entries=[80u64,448];
    let entries=entries.into_iter().enumerate().map(|(binding,size)|wgpu::BindGroupLayoutEntry{
        binding:binding as u32,visibility:wgpu::ShaderStages::VERTEX_FRAGMENT,
        ty:wgpu::BindingType::Buffer{ty:wgpu::BufferBindingType::Uniform,has_dynamic_offset:false,min_binding_size:std::num::NonZeroU64::new(size)},count:None,
    }).collect::<Vec<_>>();
    device.create_bind_group_layout(&wgpu::BindGroupLayoutDescriptor{label:Some("pick crop and clipping"),entries:&entries})
}
pub fn crop_clipping_group(device:&wgpu::Device,crop:&wgpu::Buffer,clipping:&wgpu::Buffer)->wgpu::BindGroup{
    device.create_bind_group(&wgpu::BindGroupDescriptor{label:Some("pick crop and clipping"),layout:&crop_clipping_layout(device),entries:&[
        wgpu::BindGroupEntry{binding:0,resource:crop.as_entire_binding()},
        wgpu::BindGroupEntry{binding:1,resource:clipping.as_entire_binding()},
    ]})
}

pub fn mesh(device: &wgpu::Device, view_layout: &wgpu::BindGroupLayout,
```

```rust
pub fn crop_clipping_layout(device:&wgpu::Device)->wgpu::BindGroupLayout{

    }).collect::<Vec<_>>();
    device.create_bind_group_layout(&wgpu::BindGroupLayoutDescriptor{label:Some("pick crop and clipping"),entries:&entries})
}
pub fn crop_clipping_group(device:&wgpu::Device,crop:&wgpu::Buffer,clipping:&wgpu::Buffer)->wgpu::BindGroup{
    device.create_bind_group(&wgpu::BindGroupDescriptor{label:Some("pick crop and clipping"),layout:&crop_clipping_layout(device),entries:&[
        wgpu::BindGroupEntry{binding:0,resource:crop.as_entire_binding()},
        wgpu::BindGroupEntry{binding:1,resource:clipping.as_entire_binding()},
    ]})
}

pub fn mesh(device: &wgpu::Device, view_layout: &wgpu::BindGroupLayout,
    model_layout: &wgpu::BindGroupLayout, crop_layout: &wgpu::BindGroupLayout) -> wgpu::RenderPipeline {
        let layout = device.create_pipeline_layout(&wgpu::PipelineLayoutDescriptor { label: Some("mesh IDs"),
            bind_group_layouts: &[Some(view_layout), Some(model_layout), Some(crop_layout),Some(&crate::clipping_gpu::layout(device))], immediate_size: 0 });
        let shader = device.create_shader_module(wgpu::ShaderModuleDescriptor { label: Some("mesh IDs"),
            source: wgpu::ShaderSource::Wgsl(crate::clipping_gpu::source(concat!(include_str!("triangle.wgsl"), "\n", include_str!("pick_crop.wgsl"), "\n", include_str!("triangle_pick.wgsl")),3,0).into()) });
        // An integer attachment stores exact owner IDs; opaque depth rejects hidden faces before any pixel is read back.
        device.create_render_pipeline(&wgpu::RenderPipelineDescriptor {
            label: Some("mesh IDs"), layout: Some(&layout),
            vertex: wgpu::VertexState { module: &shader, entry_point: Some("pick_vertex"), compilation_options: Default::default(),
                buffers: &[wgpu::VertexBufferLayout { array_stride: 24, step_mode: wgpu::VertexStepMode::Vertex,
                    attributes: &wgpu::vertex_attr_array![0 => Float32x3, 1 => Float32x3] },
                    wgpu::VertexBufferLayout { array_stride: 12, step_mode: wgpu::VertexStepMode::Vertex,
                        attributes: &wgpu::vertex_attr_array![2 => Float32x3] }] },
            fragment: Some(wgpu::FragmentState { module: &shader, entry_point: Some("pick_fragment"), compilation_options: Default::default(),
                targets: &[Some(wgpu::TextureFormat::Rg32Uint.into())] }), primitive: Default::default(),
```

```rust
pub fn crop_clipping_layout(device:&wgpu::Device)->wgpu::BindGroupLayout{
    let entries=[80u64,448];
    let entries=entries.into_iter().enumerate().map(|(binding,size)|wgpu::BindGroupLayoutEntry{
        binding:binding as u32,visibility:wgpu::ShaderStages::VERTEX_FRAGMENT,
        ty:wgpu::BindingType::Buffer{ty:wgpu::BufferBindingType::Uniform,has_dynamic_offset:false,min_binding_size:std::num::NonZeroU64::new(size)},count:None,
    }).collect::<Vec<_>>();
    device.create_bind_group_layout(&wgpu::BindGroupLayoutDescriptor{label:Some("pick crop and clipping"),entries:&entries})
}
pub fn crop_clipping_group(device:&wgpu::Device,crop:&wgpu::Buffer,clipping:&wgpu::Buffer)->wgpu::BindGroup{
    device.create_bind_group(&wgpu::BindGroupDescriptor{label:Some("pick crop and clipping"),layout:&crop_clipping_layout(device),entries:&[
        wgpu::BindGroupEntry{binding:0,resource:crop.as_entire_binding()},
        wgpu::BindGroupEntry{binding:1,resource:clipping.as_entire_binding()},
    ]})
}

pub fn mesh(device: &wgpu::Device, view_layout: &wgpu::BindGroupLayout,
    model_layout: &wgpu::BindGroupLayout, crop_layout: &wgpu::BindGroupLayout) -> wgpu::RenderPipeline {
        let layout = device.create_pipeline_layout(&wgpu::PipelineLayoutDescriptor { label: Some("mesh IDs"),
            bind_group_layouts: &[Some(view_layout), Some(model_layout), Some(&crop_clipping_layout(device)),Some(&crate::gpu_geometry::GpuGeometry::storage_layout(device))], immediate_size: 0 });
        let shader = device.create_shader_module(wgpu::ShaderModuleDescriptor { label: Some("mesh IDs"),
            source: wgpu::ShaderSource::Wgsl(crate::clipping_gpu::source(concat!(include_str!("triangle.wgsl"), "\n", include_str!("pick_crop.wgsl"), "\n", include_str!("triangle_pick.wgsl")),2,1).into()) });
        // An integer attachment stores exact owner IDs; opaque depth rejects hidden faces before any pixel is read back.
        device.create_render_pipeline(&wgpu::RenderPipelineDescriptor {
            label: Some("mesh IDs"), layout: Some(&layout),
            vertex: wgpu::VertexState { module: &shader, entry_point: Some("pick_vertex"), compilation_options: Default::default(),
                buffers: &[]},
            fragment: Some(wgpu::FragmentState { module: &shader, entry_point: Some("pick_fragment"), compilation_options: Default::default(),
                targets: &[Some(wgpu::TextureFormat::Rg32Uint.into()),Some(crate::primitive_image::target(true))] }), primitive: Default::default(),
            depth_stencil: Some(crate::depth::surface()), multisample: Default::default(), multiview_mask: None, cache: None,
        })
}
```

```rust
use crate::{gpu_mesh::GpuMesh, pick_window::Window, stroke_gpu::Lane};
use std::sync::{Arc, atomic::{AtomicBool, AtomicU8, Ordering}};

#[derive(Clone, Copy)]
pub struct Request { pub window: Window, pub transform: [f32; 16], pub density: f32, pub mode: crate::pick_sources::Mode }

pub(crate) struct Queued { request: Request, ticket: crate::pick_gate::Ticket, view: crate::pick_view::View }
struct Flight { ready: Arc<AtomicU8>, ticket: crate::pick_gate::Ticket, view: crate::pick_view::View }
pub enum Reply { Hit(Option<[u32; 2]>), Stale }


pub struct Picker {
    legacy_clipping:Option<crate::clipping_gpu::Clipping>,
    pipeline: wgpu::RenderPipeline,
    view: wgpu::Buffer,
    view_group: wgpu::BindGroup,
    crop: wgpu::Buffer,
    crop_group: wgpu::BindGroup,
    image: Option<crate::pick_image::Image>,
    active: Option<Flight>,
    pending: Option<Queued>,
    alive: Arc<AtomicBool>,
    gate: crate::pick_gate::Gate,
    pub submitted: u64,
    pub coalesced: u64,
}

impl Drop for Picker { fn drop(&mut self) { self.alive.store(false, Ordering::Release); self.gate.close(); } }

impl Picker {
    pub fn new(device: &wgpu::Device, view_layout: &wgpu::BindGroupLayout, model_layout: &wgpu::BindGroupLayout) -> Self {
        let crop_layout = crate::gpu_layout::uniform(device, 80);
        let pipeline = crate::pick_pipeline::mesh(device, view_layout, model_layout, &crop_layout);
        let buffer = |size| device.create_buffer(&wgpu::BufferDescriptor { label: Some("pick view"), size,
            usage: wgpu::BufferUsages::UNIFORM | wgpu::BufferUsages::COPY_DST, mapped_at_creation: false });
        let view = buffer(80); let crop = buffer(80);
        let view_group = crate::gpu_layout::group(device, view_layout, &view);
        let crop_group = crate::gpu_layout::group(device, &crop_layout, &crop);
        Self { legacy_clipping:None,pipeline, view, view_group, crop, crop_group, image: None, active: None, pending: None,
            alive: Arc::new(AtomicBool::new(true)), gate: Default::default(), submitted: 0, coalesced: 0 }
    }

    pub fn queue(&mut self, request: Request) -> Result<(), &'static str> {
        let view = crate::pick_view::View::new(request.transform, request.window.full, request.density)?;
        let ticket = self.gate.issue()?;
        if self.pending.replace(Queued { request, ticket, view }).is_some() { self.coalesced += 1; }
        Ok(())
    }

    pub fn queued(&self) -> bool { self.pending.is_some() }

    pub fn cancel(&mut self) {
        self.gate.invalidate(); self.pending = None;
        let mapped = self.active.take().is_some_and(|flight| flight.ready.load(Ordering::Acquire) <= 1);
        if let Some(image) = self.image.take() { if mapped { image.readback.unmap(); } }
    }

    pub(crate) fn next(&mut self, view: crate::pick_view::View) -> Option<Queued> {
        if self.active.is_some() { return None; }
        let queued = self.pending.take()?;
        if queued.view == view { Some(queued) } else { self.gate.invalidate(); None }
    }


    pub(crate) fn submit(&mut self, device: &wgpu::Device, queue: &wgpu::Queue, queued: Queued, meshes: &[GpuMesh], ink: &[&Lane], faces: &crate::face_pick::Faces,text:Option<&crate::scene_text_gpu::Lane>,clouds:Option<&crate::cloud_gpu::Lane>,sheets:Option<&crate::sheet_gpu::Lane>) {let clipping=self.legacy_clipping.get_or_insert_with(||crate::clipping_gpu::Clipping::new(device));let group=clipping.group.clone();let buffer=clipping.buffer.clone();self.submit_finite(device,queue,queued,meshes,ink,faces,text,clouds,sheets,None,&group,&buffer,None)}
    pub(crate) fn submit_finite(&mut self, device: &wgpu::Device, queue: &wgpu::Queue, queued: Queued, meshes: &[GpuMesh], ink: &[&Lane], faces: &crate::face_pick::Faces,text:Option<&crate::scene_text_gpu::Lane>,clouds:Option<&crate::cloud_gpu::Lane>,sheets:Option<&crate::sheet_gpu::Lane>,tables:Option<&crate::finite_ink::Tables>,clipping:&wgpu::BindGroup,clipping_buffer:&wgpu::Buffer,caps:Option<&mut crate::caps_gpu::Caps>) {
        assert!(self.active.is_none(), "Only one pick may be in flight");
        let request = queued.request; let window = request.window;
        if self.image.as_ref().is_none_or(|image| image.window.draw[2..] != window.draw[2..] || image.readback.size() != window.bytes()) {
            self.image = Some(crate::pick_image::Image::new(device, window));
        }
        let image = self.image.as_mut().unwrap(); image.window = window;
        let mut bytes: Vec<u8> = request.transform.iter().flat_map(|n| n.to_ne_bytes()).collect(); bytes.resize(80, 0);
        queue.write_buffer(&self.view, 0, &bytes);
        let mut bytes: Vec<u8> = window.crop().iter().flat_map(|n| n.to_ne_bytes()).collect();
        bytes.extend((request.mode as u32).to_ne_bytes());bytes.extend(window.draw[0].to_ne_bytes());bytes.extend(window.draw[1].to_ne_bytes());bytes.resize(80, 0); queue.write_buffer(&self.crop, 0, &bytes);
        for lane in ink { lane.view(queue, &request.transform, window.full, request.density); }
        if let Some(sheets)=sheets {sheets.view(queue,&request.transform,window.full,request.density);}
        queue.submit([crate::pick_encode::draw(device, image, window, &self.pipeline,
            &self.view_group, &self.crop_group, meshes, ink, faces, request.mode,text,clouds,sheets,&crate::text_gpu_support::GpuCtx {device:device.clone(),queue:queue.clone()},tables,clipping,caps,&self.crop,clipping_buffer)]);
        let ready = Arc::new(AtomicU8::new(0)); self.active = Some(Flight { ready: Arc::clone(&ready), ticket: queued.ticket, view: queued.view }); let alive = Arc::clone(&self.alive);
        // Mapping finishes asynchronously; the callback publishes readiness, and the owner reads the bytes without blocking browser input.
        image.readback.slice(..).map_async(wgpu::MapMode::Read, move |result| {
            ready.store(if result.is_ok() { 1 } else { 2 }, Ordering::Release);
            if alive.load(Ordering::Acquire) {
                #[cfg(target_arch = "wasm32")] crate::browser_pick::wake();
            }
        });
        self.submitted += 1;
    }

    pub fn take(&mut self, view: crate::pick_view::View) -> Option<Result<Reply, &'static str>> {
        let status = self.active.as_ref()?.ready.load(Ordering::Acquire);
        if status == 0 { return None; }
        let flight = self.active.take().unwrap();
        let current = self.gate.accept(flight.ticket) && flight.view == view;
        let image = self.image.as_ref().unwrap();
        if status != 1 { return Some(if current { Err("Cannot read GPU pick image") } else { Ok(Reply::Stale) }); }
        let bytes = image.readback.slice(..).get_mapped_range();
        let result = if current { image.window.closest(&bytes).map(Reply::Hit) } else { Ok(Reply::Stale) };
        drop(bytes); image.readback.unmap(); Some(result)
    }

    pub fn usage(&self) -> [u64; 3] {
        [u64::from(self.active.is_some()), u64::from(self.pending.is_some()), self.image.as_ref().map_or(0, |image|
            image.readback.size() + u64::from(image.window.draw[2]) * u64::from(image.window.draw[3]) * 24) + 160+u64::from(self.legacy_clipping.is_some())*448]
    }
}
```

### 5. `src/pick_encode.rs`

Receive both existing uniforms at the encoder boundary. Build only the combined binding, retaining independent crop groups for other owners. Clear and store the bounded primitive image in the same mesh depth test. Geometry owns group three in this mesh pass. Use the combined crop/clipping group only for mesh picking. Match nonindexed occurrences with the shared index reader. Source-face selection moves into its own integer color pass. Retain underlying mesh depth and primitive addresses while replacing the owner payload with a selected source-face ID. The ink predicate receives the bounded primitive image of this exact pick request.

```rust
use crate::{gpu_mesh::GpuMesh, pick_image::Image, pick_window::Window, stroke_gpu::Lane};

pub(crate) fn draw(device: &wgpu::Device, image: &Image, window: Window, pipeline: &wgpu::RenderPipeline,
    view_group: &wgpu::BindGroup, crop_group: &wgpu::BindGroup, meshes: &[GpuMesh], ink: &[&Lane], faces: &crate::face_pick::Faces, mode: crate::pick_sources::Mode,text:Option<&crate::scene_text_gpu::Lane>,clouds:Option<&crate::cloud_gpu::Lane>,sheets:Option<&crate::sheet_gpu::Lane>,ctx:&crate::text_gpu_support::GpuCtx,tables:Option<&crate::finite_ink::Tables>,clipping:&wgpu::BindGroup,caps:Option<&mut crate::caps_gpu::Caps>,crop:&wgpu::Buffer) -> wgpu::CommandBuffer {
        let mut encoder = device.create_command_encoder(&Default::default());
        {let _clear=encoder.begin_render_pass(&wgpu::RenderPassDescriptor{label:Some("clear bounded section depth"),color_attachments:&[Some(wgpu::RenderPassColorAttachment{view:&image.section,depth_slice:None,resolve_target:None,ops:wgpu::Operations{load:wgpu::LoadOp::Clear(wgpu::Color::TRANSPARENT),store:wgpu::StoreOp::Store}})],..Default::default()});}
        {
            let mut pass = encoder.begin_render_pass(&wgpu::RenderPassDescriptor { label: Some("IDs"),
                color_attachments: &[Some(wgpu::RenderPassColorAttachment { view: &image.colour, depth_slice: None, resolve_target: None,
                    ops: wgpu::Operations { load: wgpu::LoadOp::Clear(wgpu::Color::TRANSPARENT), store: wgpu::StoreOp::Store } })],
                depth_stencil_attachment: Some(wgpu::RenderPassDepthStencilAttachment { view: &image.depth,
                    depth_ops: Some(wgpu::Operations { load: wgpu::LoadOp::Clear(1.0), store: wgpu::StoreOp::Store }), stencil_ops: None }), ..Default::default() });
            pass.set_bind_group(3,clipping,&[]);
            pass.set_pipeline(pipeline); pass.set_bind_group(0, view_group, &[]); pass.set_bind_group(2, crop_group, &[]);
            for mesh in meshes { mesh.draw(&mut pass); }
            if mode == crate::pick_sources::Mode::Face { faces.draw(&mut pass, meshes, view_group, crop_group); }
        }
        if let Some(caps)=caps{caps.encode_pick(device,&ctx.queue,&mut encoder,image,window,view_group,crop,clipping,meshes,mode);}
        let tables=tables.map(|tables|crate::finite_ink::Tables{projected:tables.projected.clone(),tiles:tables.tiles.clone(),caps:image.section.clone(),primitive:tables.primitive.clone()});
        {
            let mut pass=encoder.begin_render_pass(&wgpu::RenderPassDescriptor{label:Some("finite integer ink"),color_attachments:&[Some(wgpu::RenderPassColorAttachment{view:&image.colour,depth_slice:None,resolve_target:None,ops:wgpu::Operations{load:wgpu::LoadOp::Load,store:wgpu::StoreOp::Store}})],depth_stencil_attachment:Some(wgpu::RenderPassDepthStencilAttachment{view:&image.depth,depth_ops:None,stencil_ops:None}),..Default::default()});
            pass.set_bind_group(3,clipping,&[]);
            for lane in ink {lane.pick_finite(device,&mut pass,crop_group,tables.as_ref(),&image.depth);}
        }
        {
            let mut pass=encoder.begin_render_pass(&wgpu::RenderPassDescriptor{label:Some("other original integer owners"),color_attachments:&[Some(wgpu::RenderPassColorAttachment{view:&image.colour,depth_slice:None,resolve_target:None,ops:wgpu::Operations{load:wgpu::LoadOp::Load,store:wgpu::StoreOp::Store}})],depth_stencil_attachment:Some(wgpu::RenderPassDepthStencilAttachment{view:&image.depth,depth_ops:Some(wgpu::Operations{load:wgpu::LoadOp::Load,store:wgpu::StoreOp::Store}),stencil_ops:None}),..Default::default()});
            if let Some(clouds)=clouds {clouds.pick(ctx,&mut pass,window,mode);}
            if let Some(sheets)=sheets {sheets.pick(&mut pass,crop_group,clipping);}
            if let Some(text)=text {text.draw_ids(&mut pass,crop_group);}
        }
        // The surrounding halo supports rasterization; only the smaller cursor window is copied back for hit testing.
        encoder.copy_texture_to_buffer(wgpu::TexelCopyTextureInfo { texture: &image.texture, mip_level: 0,
            origin: wgpu::Origin3d { x: window.copy[0] - window.draw[0], y: window.copy[1] - window.draw[1], z: 0 }, aspect: wgpu::TextureAspect::All },
            wgpu::TexelCopyBufferInfo { buffer: &image.readback, layout: wgpu::TexelCopyBufferLayout { offset: 0,
                bytes_per_row: Some(window.row_bytes()), rows_per_image: Some(window.copy[3]) } },
            wgpu::Extent3d { width: window.copy[2], height: window.copy[3], depth_or_array_layers: 1 });
        encoder.finish()
}
```

```rust
use crate::{gpu_mesh::GpuMesh, pick_image::Image, pick_window::Window, stroke_gpu::Lane};

pub(crate) fn draw(device: &wgpu::Device, image: &Image, window: Window, pipeline: &wgpu::RenderPipeline,
    view_group: &wgpu::BindGroup, crop_group: &wgpu::BindGroup, meshes: &[GpuMesh], ink: &[&Lane], faces: &crate::face_pick::Faces, mode: crate::pick_sources::Mode,text:Option<&crate::scene_text_gpu::Lane>,clouds:Option<&crate::cloud_gpu::Lane>,sheets:Option<&crate::sheet_gpu::Lane>,ctx:&crate::text_gpu_support::GpuCtx,tables:Option<&crate::finite_ink::Tables>,clipping:&wgpu::BindGroup,caps:Option<&mut crate::caps_gpu::Caps>,crop:&wgpu::Buffer,clipping_buffer:&wgpu::Buffer) -> wgpu::CommandBuffer {
        let mut encoder = device.create_command_encoder(&Default::default());
        let mesh_crop=crate::pick_pipeline::crop_clipping_group(device,crop,clipping_buffer);
        {let _clear=encoder.begin_render_pass(&wgpu::RenderPassDescriptor{label:Some("clear bounded section depth"),color_attachments:&[Some(wgpu::RenderPassColorAttachment{view:&image.section,depth_slice:None,resolve_target:None,ops:wgpu::Operations{load:wgpu::LoadOp::Clear(wgpu::Color::TRANSPARENT),store:wgpu::StoreOp::Store}})],..Default::default()});}
        {
            let mut pass = encoder.begin_render_pass(&wgpu::RenderPassDescriptor { label: Some("IDs"),
                color_attachments: &[Some(wgpu::RenderPassColorAttachment { view: &image.colour, depth_slice: None, resolve_target: None,
                    ops: wgpu::Operations { load: wgpu::LoadOp::Clear(wgpu::Color::TRANSPARENT), store: wgpu::StoreOp::Store } }),Some(wgpu::RenderPassColorAttachment{view:&image.primitive.view,depth_slice:None,resolve_target:None,ops:wgpu::Operations{load:wgpu::LoadOp::Clear(wgpu::Color::TRANSPARENT),store:wgpu::StoreOp::Store}})],
                depth_stencil_attachment: Some(wgpu::RenderPassDepthStencilAttachment { view: &image.depth,
                    depth_ops: Some(wgpu::Operations { load: wgpu::LoadOp::Clear(1.0), store: wgpu::StoreOp::Store }), stencil_ops: None }), ..Default::default() });
            pass.set_pipeline(pipeline); pass.set_bind_group(0, view_group, &[]); pass.set_bind_group(2, &mesh_crop, &[]);
            for mesh in meshes { mesh.draw_pulled(&mut pass); }

        }
        if mode==crate::pick_sources::Mode::Face{
            let mut pass=encoder.begin_render_pass(&wgpu::RenderPassDescriptor{label:Some("original source-face IDs"),color_attachments:&[Some(wgpu::RenderPassColorAttachment{view:&image.colour,depth_slice:None,resolve_target:None,ops:wgpu::Operations{load:wgpu::LoadOp::Load,store:wgpu::StoreOp::Store}})],depth_stencil_attachment:Some(wgpu::RenderPassDepthStencilAttachment{view:&image.depth,depth_ops:None,stencil_ops:None}),..Default::default()});
            faces.draw(&mut pass,meshes,view_group,crop_group);
        }
        if let Some(caps)=caps{caps.encode_pick(device,&ctx.queue,&mut encoder,image,window,view_group,crop,clipping,meshes,mode);}
        let tables=tables.map(|tables|crate::finite_ink::Tables{projected:tables.projected.clone(),tiles:tables.tiles.clone(),caps:image.section.clone(),primitive:image.primitive.view.clone()});
        {
            let mut pass=encoder.begin_render_pass(&wgpu::RenderPassDescriptor{label:Some("finite integer ink"),color_attachments:&[Some(wgpu::RenderPassColorAttachment{view:&image.colour,depth_slice:None,resolve_target:None,ops:wgpu::Operations{load:wgpu::LoadOp::Load,store:wgpu::StoreOp::Store}})],depth_stencil_attachment:Some(wgpu::RenderPassDepthStencilAttachment{view:&image.depth,depth_ops:None,stencil_ops:None}),..Default::default()});
            pass.set_bind_group(3,clipping,&[]);
            for lane in ink {lane.pick_finite(device,&mut pass,crop_group,tables.as_ref(),&image.depth);}
        }
        {
            let mut pass=encoder.begin_render_pass(&wgpu::RenderPassDescriptor{label:Some("other original integer owners"),color_attachments:&[Some(wgpu::RenderPassColorAttachment{view:&image.colour,depth_slice:None,resolve_target:None,ops:wgpu::Operations{load:wgpu::LoadOp::Load,store:wgpu::StoreOp::Store}})],depth_stencil_attachment:Some(wgpu::RenderPassDepthStencilAttachment{view:&image.depth,depth_ops:Some(wgpu::Operations{load:wgpu::LoadOp::Load,store:wgpu::StoreOp::Store}),stencil_ops:None}),..Default::default()});
            if let Some(clouds)=clouds {clouds.pick(ctx,&mut pass,window,mode);}
            if let Some(sheets)=sheets {sheets.pick(&mut pass,crop_group,clipping);}
            if let Some(text)=text {text.draw_ids(&mut pass,crop_group);}
        }
        // The surrounding halo supports rasterization; only the smaller cursor window is copied back for hit testing.
        encoder.copy_texture_to_buffer(wgpu::TexelCopyTextureInfo { texture: &image.texture, mip_level: 0,
            origin: wgpu::Origin3d { x: window.copy[0] - window.draw[0], y: window.copy[1] - window.draw[1], z: 0 }, aspect: wgpu::TextureAspect::All },
            wgpu::TexelCopyBufferInfo { buffer: &image.readback, layout: wgpu::TexelCopyBufferLayout { offset: 0,
                bytes_per_row: Some(window.row_bytes()), rows_per_image: Some(window.copy[3]) } },
            wgpu::Extent3d { width: window.copy[2], height: window.copy[3], depth_or_array_layers: 1 });
        encoder.finish()
}
```

<a id="checkpoint-96zt-fit-surface-depth-beneath-a-point-disc"></a>

### Step 13 of 21 · Fit surface depth beneath a point disc (`96zt-fit-surface-depth-beneath-a-point-disc`)

- **Goal:** Type the current two-axis surface carry for a disc fragment.
- **Data Flow Trace:** `Follow gpu_geometry.rs, triangle.wgsl, gpu_mesh.rs, instance_gpu.rs and renderer.rs in this cumulative checkpoint; compare the maintained engine/gpu/ and shaders/triangle.wgsl ownership.`
- **Files You Touch:** `src/ink_plane.wgsl` (*Type the current two-axis disc-plane carry while reusing the existing neighbor and tolerance helpers.*)

A point has no stroke direction.

Fit depth independently along the screen x and y axes, trying the neighbor away from the center first. Carry the sampled plane to the disc center and reuse the same depth tolerance and visibility rule as the stroke. If either axis cannot form a planar pair, use the conservative raw-depth comparison. This preparatory helper does not change marker visibility yet.

> **Self-Check Question:** *Why does a disc need two fitted slopes?*  
> **Answer:** Its covered pixels surround the center in every direction. One slope cannot carry an arbitrary fragment position to that center.

- **Hands-On Lab Experiment:** Work through a point on a sloping face and a point behind it. Compile the helper while the existing point picture remains unchanged.

```wgsl
    let z_side = ink_depth(pixel + side, sample);
    let e = axis.at - pixel;
    let det = axis.along.x * side.y - axis.along.y * side.x;
    let a = (e.x * side.y - e.y * side.x) / det;
    let b = (axis.along.x * e.y - axis.along.y * e.x) / det;
    let g = z_side - z;
    let predicted = z + a * axis.slope + b * g;
    let tolerance = ink_tolerance(axis.depth, abs(g) + abs(axis.slope), abs(b)) + ink_sag_slack(axis.sag, vec2<f32>(g, axis.slope));
    return ink_carry_visible(predicted, z, axis.depth, tolerance);
}
```

```wgsl
fn ink_disc_fragment_visible(pixel: vec2<f32>, centre: vec2<f32>, depth: f32, sample: u32) -> bool {
    let z = ink_depth(pixel, sample);
    if (z == 0.0) {
        return true;
    }
    let d = pixel - centre;
    var along_x = vec2<f32>(select(1.0, -1.0, d.x < 0.0), 0.0);
    var along_y = vec2<f32>(0.0, select(1.0, -1.0, d.y < 0.0));
    if (!ink_pair_planar(pixel, along_x, z, sample)) {
        along_x = -along_x;
        if (!ink_pair_planar(pixel, along_x, z, sample)) {
            return z <= depth + abs(depth) * DEPTH_REL_TOL;
        }
    }
    if (!ink_pair_planar(pixel, along_y, z, sample)) {
        along_y = -along_y;
        if (!ink_pair_planar(pixel, along_y, z, sample)) {
            return z <= depth + abs(depth) * DEPTH_REL_TOL;
        }
    }
    let gx = (ink_depth(pixel + along_x, sample) - z) * along_x.x;
    let gy = (ink_depth(pixel + along_y, sample) - z) * along_y.y;
    let predicted = z - d.x * gx - d.y * gy;
    return ink_carry_visible(predicted, z, depth, ink_tolerance(depth, abs(gx) + abs(gy), abs(d.x) + abs(d.y)));
}
```

<a id="checkpoint-96zu-test-the-disc-center-against-a-surface"></a>

### Step 14 of 21 · Test the disc center against a surface (`96zu-test-the-disc-center-against-a-surface`)

- **Goal:** Type the current center check that prevents a hidden point leaking at a fringe.
- **Data Flow Trace:** `Follow gpu_geometry.rs, triangle.wgsl, gpu_mesh.rs, instance_gpu.rs and renderer.rs in this cumulative checkpoint; compare the maintained engine/gpu/ and shaders/triangle.wgsl ownership.`
- **Files You Touch:** `src/ink_plane.wgsl` (*Type the current source-center occlusion check and reuse it with fragment visibility.*)

A fragment beside an occluder may be exposed even though the original point center is behind it.

Inspect the center pixel at the fragment’s subpixel phase, try every neighbor quadrant and require the diagonal to agree with the fitted plane. A matching nearer plane hides the point. Combine this center check with the previous fragment test. Keep the helpers inactive until marker callers connect.

> **Self-Check Question:** *Why check the diagonal too?*  
> **Answer:** An edge or corner can make horizontal and vertical pairs look plausible individually. The diagonal tests whether they belong to one plane.

- **Hands-On Lab Experiment:** Sketch a point center hidden just behind a face edge. Explain which quadrant can prove that the center remains occluded.

```wgsl
fn ink_disc_source_hidden(pixel: vec2<f32>, centre: vec2<f32>, depth: f32, sample: u32) -> bool {
    let z = ink_depth(pixel, sample);
    if (z == 0.0) {
        return false;
    }
    let d = pixel - centre;
    for (var x = 0u; x < 2u; x++) {
        let dx = vec2<f32>(select(1.0, -1.0, x == 1u), 0.0);
        if (!ink_pair_planar(pixel, dx, z, sample)) {
            continue;
        }
        let gx = (ink_depth(pixel + dx, sample) - z) * dx.x;
        for (var y = 0u; y < 2u; y++) {
            let dy = vec2<f32>(0.0, select(1.0, -1.0, y == 1u));
            if (!ink_pair_planar(pixel, dy, z, sample)) {
                continue;
            }
            let gy = (ink_depth(pixel + dy, sample) - z) * dy.y;
            let diagonal = ink_depth(pixel + dx + dy, sample);
            let expected = z + gx * dx.x + gy * dy.y;
            if (diagonal == 0.0 || abs(diagonal - expected) > ink_tolerance(z, abs(gx) + abs(gy), 2.0)) {
                continue;
            }
            let predicted = z - d.x * gx - d.y * gy;
            if (predicted > depth + ink_tolerance(depth, abs(gx) + abs(gy), abs(d.x) + abs(d.y))) {
                return true;
            }
        }
    }
    return false;
}
fn ink_disc_visible(pixel: vec2<f32>, centre: vec2<f32>, depth: f32, sample: u32) -> bool {
    if (!ink_disc_fragment_visible(pixel, centre, depth, sample)) {
        return false;
    }
    let source_pixel = floor(centre) + fract(pixel);
    return !ink_disc_source_hidden(source_pixel, centre, depth, sample);
}
```

### Step 15 of 21 · Carry the rasterized triangle gradient to ink (`96zv-carry-the-rasterized-triangle-gradient-to-ink`)

- **Goal:** Read the lossless primitive address and use its projected surface gradient.
- **Data Flow Trace:** `Follow gpu_geometry.rs, triangle.wgsl, gpu_mesh.rs, instance_gpu.rs and renderer.rs in this cumulative checkpoint; compare the maintained engine/gpu/ and shaders/triangle.wgsl ownership.`
- **Files You Touch:** `src/ink_plane.wgsl` (*Type the current gradient carry with the matching canvas-or-crop primitive reader.*)

The color and first-pick passes now produce matching depth and primitive images.

Read the unsigned halves at the same pixel and recover the one-based triangle address. Its projected record supplies a depth gradient, so carrying the surface to the stroke needs no neighbor fit when that gradient is valid. Missing or excessively steep records use the existing neighbor fallback. Keep the current slope and sag error budget. This preparatory boundary still leaves the existing finite-visibility callers unchanged.

> **Self-Check Question:** *Why must the primitive and depth readers use the same pixel mapping?*  
> **Answer:** Color uses canvas pixels while first picking uses a bounded crop. Mixing their origins would fit the wrong triangle even though the integer address itself is exact.

- **Hands-On Lab Experiment:** Compare a normal projected triangle with a near-clipped or grazing triangle. Identify when the explicit gradient or neighbor fallback applies.

```wgsl
fn ink_primitive(pixel:vec2<f32>,sample:u32)->u32{
    let at=finite_depth_pixel(pixel);let size=vec2<i32>(textureDimensions(finite_primitive));
    if(any(at<vec2<i32>(0))||any(at>=size)){return 0u;}
    let halves=textureLoad(finite_primitive,at,0).xy;
    return halves.x|(halves.y<<16u);
}
fn ink_visible_plane(pixel: vec2<f32>, axis: InkAxis, sample: u32) -> bool {
    let z = ink_depth(pixel, sample);
    if (z == 0.0) {
        return true;
    }
    let primitive = ink_primitive(pixel, sample);
    var gradient = vec2<f32>(0.0);
    if (primitive != 0u) {
        let triangle = min(primitive, arrayLength(&finite_projected)) - 1u;
        if (finite_projected[triangle].edge3.w < 3.0) {
            return ink_axis_visible(pixel, axis, sample);
        }
        gradient = finite_projected[triangle].gradient.xy;
    }
    if (any(abs(gradient) >= vec2<f32>(SLOPE_INVALID))) {
        return ink_axis_visible(pixel, axis, sample);
    }
    let delta = axis.at - pixel;
    let predicted = z + dot(gradient, delta);
    let tolerance = ink_tolerance(axis.depth, abs(gradient.x)+abs(gradient.y), abs(delta.x)+abs(delta.y)) + ink_sag_slack(axis.sag, gradient);
    return ink_carry_visible(predicted, z, axis.depth, tolerance);
}
```

### Step 16 of 21 · Connect surface-fitted point visibility (`96zw-connect-surface-fitted-point-visibility`)

- **Goal:** Use the disc fragment and center tests for original point color and integer picks.
- **Data Flow Trace:** `Original point → projected disc → surface fit and center check → color and cropped owner pick.`
- **Files You Touch:** `src/finite_visibility.wgsl` (*Connect the point-specific surface and center tests after clipping and section checks.*) · `src/finite_marker.wgsl` (*Use the same original point predicate for color and cropped integer ownership.*) · `src/finite_marker_pick.wgsl` (*Use the same original point predicate for color and cropped integer ownership.*) · `src/finite_visibility_tests.rs` (*Supply an actual point disc pixel and first-pick regression at both physical display scales.*)

A point is a disc, so a stroke axis cannot describe its visibility.

Connect the two-axis surface fit and the center occlusion check prepared in the preceding steps. Use the same function in color and cropped integer picking. Keep the section and clipping checks, and preserve hidden-ink alpha through translucent faces. The camera still uses the declared forward-depth conversion; ray-facing discs and multisampling remain later steps.

> **Self-Check Question:** *Why must the center test run in integer picking too?*  
> **Answer:** A visible fringe of a point whose center is behind a face must not win a pick. The same disc predicate keeps color and ownership consistent.

- **What You Should See:** A visible point retains its full CSS disc on a sloping face. A hidden point paints no opaque ink and cannot win the first pick.
- **Hands-On Lab Experiment:** Compare a point on a sloping face with a hidden point. Pick before drawing any color frame, repeat at both display scales, and verify that the original point owner wins only when visible.

```wgsl
fn finite_disc_alpha(pixel:vec2<f32>,centre:vec2<f32>,depth:f32,flags:u32)->f32{
 if(clip_active()&&clip_cut_ink_ndc(flags,clip_ndc(centre,view.size,1.-depth))){return 0.;}
 let tolerance=abs(depth)*DEPTH_REL_TOL+abs(1.-depth)*2.3841858e-7;

 return select(1.,select(0.,1.-view.opacity,glass),hidden&&view.opacity>0.);
}
```

<a id="checkpoint-96zx-test-the-rasterized-neighbors-at-the-stroke-source"></a>

### Step 17 of 21 · Test rasterized neighbors at the stroke source (`96zx-test-the-rasterized-neighbors-at-the-stroke-source`)

- **Goal:** Prepare exact source tests using the matching primitive image.
- **Data Flow Trace:** `Projected stroke axis → primitive image → exact source hit and neighboring triangle hits`
- **Files You Touch:** `src/ink_plane.wgsl` (*Build a weighted source axis and reuse one exact clipped-triangle predicate for fringe and source neighbors.*)

A thick stroke may touch a triangle outside its center line.

After projection, depth is affine along the screen segment. Weight each endpoint by its distance to the other end, avoiding cancellation when a near-clipped endpoint lies far off screen. Read the triangle at its coverage pixel and the four pixels surrounding the source. Test each projected triangle at the exact center line, using the current depth floor and clipping predicate. Reject empty or out-of-range primitive addresses. Keep this helper separate from tile traversal so the same exact hit rule is used once. This stage prepares the rule without changing active stroke visibility.

> **Self-Check Question:** *Why test the exact center line after fitting a rasterized surface?*  
> **Answer:** Coverage pixels can lie beside the center line or across a face edge. Exact projected triangles settle whether the source itself is occluded.

- **What You Should See:** The view stays unchanged while the exact source helpers compile. Original geometry and integer owners remain unchanged.
- **Hands-On Lab Experiment:** Trace one center-line sample across a projected face edge and compare its original triangle address with the rasterized neighbor. The browser picture stays unchanged until the [visibility connection](../../96zy-connect-fitted-surfaces-and-exact-stroke-occlusion.md).

```wgsl
fn finite_ink_axis(pixel:vec2<f32>,ends:vec4<f32>,depths:vec2<f32>)->InkAxis{
 let delta=ends.zw-ends.xy;let len=length(delta);let along=delta/max(len,1e-6);
 let distance=clamp(dot(pixel-ends.xy,along),0.,len);let at=ends.xy+along*distance;
 let depth=select(depths.x,(depths.x*(len-distance)+depths.y*distance)/max(len,1e-6),len>1e-6);
 return InkAxis(at,depth,along,(depths.y-depths.x)/max(len,1e-3),vec2<f32>(0.));
}
fn ink_exact_floor(axis:InkAxis,triangle:ProjectedTriangle)->f32{
 return axis.depth+abs(axis.depth)*DEPTH_REL_TOL+ink_sag_slack(axis.sag,triangle.gradient.xy);
}
fn finite_triangle_hides(primitive:u32,axis:InkAxis)->bool{
 if(primitive==0u||primitive>arrayLength(&finite_projected)){return false;}
 let triangle=finite_projected[primitive-1u];let hit=projected_triangle_at(triangle,axis.at);
 return hit.y>0.5&&hit.x>ink_exact_floor(axis,triangle)&&!(clip_active()&&clip_cut_ndc(0u,clip_ndc(axis.at,view.size,1.-hit.x)));
}
fn finite_source_hidden(pixel:vec2<f32>,axis:InkAxis)->bool{
 let fringe=ink_primitive(pixel,0u);if(finite_triangle_hides(fringe,axis)){return true;}
 let base=floor(axis.at-fract(pixel))+fract(pixel);
 for(var i=0u;i<4u;i++){
  let primitive=ink_primitive(base+vec2<f32>(f32(i&1u),f32(i>>1u)),0u);
  if(primitive!=fringe&&finite_triangle_hides(primitive,axis)){return true;}
 }
 return false;
}
```

<a id="checkpoint-96zy-connect-fitted-surfaces-and-exact-stroke-occlusion"></a>

### Step 18 of 21 · Connect fitted surfaces and exact stroke occlusion (`96zy-connect-fitted-surfaces-and-exact-stroke-occlusion`)

- **Goal:** Activate surface fitting, source-neighbor tests and bounded tile fallback for original strokes.
- **Data Flow Trace:** `Stroke axis → surface fit and section check → exact source hits → bounded tile fallback`
- **Files You Touch:** `src/finite_visibility.wgsl` (*Retain the strict construction-grid rule while original strokes use the current fitted and exact predicates.*) · `src/ink_plane.wgsl` (*Retain the declared forward-depth rounding allowance in exact source tests until the reversed camera replaces this adapter.*)

Build an InkAxis from the original segment and fit the physical triangle under its coverage pixel.

A cap under the source is checked on its clipping plane; a cap only beside the source makes the source pixel decide the fit. Test the fringe and source neighbors exactly before accepting a fitted surface. If the fit rejects the stroke, traverse its bounded tile list with the same exact triangle predicate. An absent list leaves the fit in charge; an incomplete or overflowing list conservatively hides the stroke. Keep the strict construction-grid rule separately: coplanar grid lines must remain hidden. Source sag is still zero, so curved source-edge slack needs its next data bridge.

The current helpers use reversed depth, while this checkpoint still converts a forward-depth image. Carry its existing two-float-rounding allowance into the exact depth floor. The allowance follows the represented forward depth; it is not a world-space offset. This prevents coplanar original surface and CAD edges from hiding themselves at the far end of the teaching camera range. Remove the conversion allowance when the reversed camera connects.

> **Self-Check Question:** *What supplies visibility when a projected tile list is unavailable?*  
> **Answer:** The bounded physical-depth fallback remains available. Otherwise the [source-neighbor helper](../../96zx-test-the-rasterized-neighbors-at-the-stroke-source.md) resolves the original center line against projected triangles.

- **What You Should See:** Coplanar ordinary strokes retain their CSS width. With opaque faces, front strokes draw and pick their original owner; hidden strokes do neither.
- **Hands-On Lab Experiment:** Create a Box and a Line on one face. Orbit to a grazing view: the coplanar line keeps its CSS width. Move the line behind an opaque face; it must neither draw nor win the original pick. Undo the move.

```wgsl
// The tutorial still converts forward depth; allow two float rounding units until the reversed camera connects.
const FINITE_GRID:bool=false;
@group(1) @binding(0) var<storage,read> finite_projected:array<ProjectedTriangle>;
@group(1) @binding(1) var<storage,read> finite_tiles:array<vec4<u32>>;
@group(1) @binding(2) var finite_depth:texture_depth_2d;
@group(1) @binding(3) var finite_sections:texture_2d<f32>;
@group(1) @binding(4) var finite_primitive:texture_2d<u32>;
#include "ink_plane.wgsl"
fn finite_section(pixel:vec2<f32>,depth:f32,tolerance:f32)->bool{
 let at=finite_depth_pixel(pixel);let size=vec2<i32>(textureDimensions(finite_sections));if(any(at<vec2<i32>(0))||any(at>=size)){return true;}let cap=textureLoad(finite_sections,at,0).xy;if(cap.y<0.5){return true;}let plane=(u32(cap.y)-1u)&7u;let z=1.0-clip_plane_depth(plane,clip_ndc(pixel,view.size,0.0).xy);return select(z<=depth+tolerance,z<depth-tolerance,FINITE_GRID);
}
fn finite_physical(pixel:vec2<f32>,depth:f32,tolerance:f32)->bool{let at=finite_depth_pixel(pixel);let size=vec2<i32>(textureDimensions(finite_depth));if(any(at<vec2<i32>(0))||any(at>=size)){return false;}let z=1.0-textureLoad(finite_depth,at,0);return select(z<=depth+tolerance,z<depth-tolerance,FINITE_GRID);}
fn finite_screen(p:vec4<f32>)->vec2<f32>{return(p.xy/p.w*vec2<f32>(0.5,-0.5)+0.5)*view.size;}
fn finite_axis(a:vec4<f32>,b:vec4<f32>)->vec4<f32>{return vec4<f32>(finite_screen(a),finite_screen(b));}
fn finite_visible(pixel:vec2<f32>,axis:vec4<f32>,depths:vec2<f32>,flags:u32)->bool{
 let delta=axis.zw-axis.xy;let length2=dot(delta,delta);let t=clamp(dot(pixel-axis.xy,delta)/max(length2,1e-20),0.0,1.0);let at=axis.xy+t*delta;let depth=mix(depths.x,depths.y,t);let relative=abs(depth)*1.9073486e-6+abs(1.0-depth)*2.3841858e-7;
 if(!finite_section(at,depth,relative)){return false;}
 if(clip_active()&&clip_cut_ink_ndc(flags,clip_ndc(at,view.size,1.0-depth))){return false;}
 if(any(at<vec2<f32>(0.0))||any(at>=view.size)){return finite_physical(pixel,depth,relative);}
 if(finite_tiles[0].x==0u){return finite_physical(pixel,depth,relative);}
 let span=f32(visibility_tile_span_of(u32(view.size.x),u32(view.size.y)));let size=vec2<u32>(ceil(view.size/span));let cell=vec2<u32>(at/span);let head=finite_tiles[1u+cell.y*size.x+cell.x];
 if(head.w!=0u||head.z!=head.x){return finite_physical(pixel,depth,relative);}
 for(var i=0u;i<head.x;i++){
  let offset=head.y+i*2u;let nearest=bitcast<f32>(finite_tiles[(offset+1u)/4u][(offset+1u)%4u]);if(nearest<depth-relative||(!FINITE_GRID&&nearest<=depth+relative)){continue;}
  let primitive=finite_tiles[offset/4u][offset%4u];if(primitive==0u||primitive>arrayLength(&finite_projected)){continue;}
  let triangle=finite_projected[primitive-1u];let hit=projected_triangle_at(triangle,at);let tolerance=relative+0.00390625*(abs(triangle.gradient.x)+abs(triangle.gradient.y));
  if(hit.y>0.5&&!(clip_active()&&clip_cut_ndc(0u,clip_ndc(at,view.size,1.0-hit.x)))&&select(hit.x>depth+tolerance,hit.x>=depth-tolerance,FINITE_GRID)){return false;}
 }
 return true;
}


fn finite_alpha(pixel:vec2<f32>,axis:vec4<f32>,depths:vec2<f32>,flags:u32)->f32{
 let delta=axis.zw-axis.xy;let t=clamp(dot(pixel-axis.xy,delta)/max(dot(delta,delta),1e-20),0.,1.);let at=axis.xy+t*delta;let depth=mix(depths.x,depths.y,t);
 if(clip_active()&&clip_cut_ink_ndc(flags,clip_ndc(at,view.size,1.-depth))){return 0.;}
 let hidden=!finite_visible(pixel,axis,depths,flags);let glass=view.opacity>0.&&view.opacity<1.;return select(1.,select(0.,1.-view.opacity,glass),hidden&&view.opacity>0.);
}

fn finite_disc_alpha(pixel:vec2<f32>,centre:vec2<f32>,depth:f32,flags:u32)->f32{
 if(clip_active()&&clip_cut_ink_ndc(flags,clip_ndc(centre,view.size,1.-depth))){return 0.;}
 let tolerance=abs(depth)*DEPTH_REL_TOL+abs(1.-depth)*2.3841858e-7;
 let hidden=!finite_section(centre,depth,tolerance)||!ink_disc_visible(pixel,centre,depth,0u);
 let glass=view.opacity>0.&&view.opacity<1.;
 return select(1.,select(0.,1.-view.opacity,glass),hidden&&view.opacity>0.);
}
```

```wgsl
const FINITE_GRID:bool=false;
@group(1) @binding(0) var<storage,read> finite_projected:array<ProjectedTriangle>;

@group(1) @binding(2) var finite_depth:texture_depth_2d;
@group(1) @binding(3) var finite_sections:texture_2d<f32>;
@group(1) @binding(4) var finite_primitive:texture_2d<u32>;
#include "ink_plane.wgsl"
fn finite_section(pixel:vec2<f32>,depth:f32,tolerance:f32)->bool{
 let at=finite_depth_pixel(pixel);let size=vec2<i32>(textureDimensions(finite_sections));if(any(at<vec2<i32>(0))||any(at>=size)){return true;}let cap=textureLoad(finite_sections,at,0).xy;if(cap.y<0.5){return true;}let plane=(u32(cap.y)-1u)&7u;let z=1.0-clip_plane_depth(plane,clip_ndc(pixel,view.size,0.0).xy);return select(z<=depth+tolerance,z<depth-tolerance,FINITE_GRID);
}
fn finite_physical(pixel:vec2<f32>,depth:f32,tolerance:f32)->bool{let at=finite_depth_pixel(pixel);let size=vec2<i32>(textureDimensions(finite_depth));if(any(at<vec2<i32>(0))||any(at>=size)){return false;}let z=1.0-textureLoad(finite_depth,at,0);return select(z<=depth+tolerance,z<depth-tolerance,FINITE_GRID);}
fn finite_screen(p:vec4<f32>)->vec2<f32>{return(p.xy/p.w*vec2<f32>(0.5,-0.5)+0.5)*view.size;}
fn finite_axis(a:vec4<f32>,b:vec4<f32>)->vec4<f32>{return vec4<f32>(finite_screen(a),finite_screen(b));}
fn finite_grid_visible(pixel:vec2<f32>,axis:vec4<f32>,depths:vec2<f32>,flags:u32)->bool{
 let delta=axis.zw-axis.xy;let length2=dot(delta,delta);let t=clamp(dot(pixel-axis.xy,delta)/max(length2,1e-20),0.0,1.0);let at=axis.xy+t*delta;let depth=mix(depths.x,depths.y,t);let relative=abs(depth)*1.9073486e-6+abs(1.0-depth)*2.3841858e-7;
 if(!finite_section(at,depth,relative)){return false;}
 if(clip_active()&&clip_cut_ink_ndc(flags,clip_ndc(at,view.size,1.0-depth))){return false;}
 if(any(at<vec2<f32>(0.0))||any(at>=view.size)){return finite_physical(pixel,depth,relative);}
 if(finite_tiles[0].x==0u){return finite_physical(pixel,depth,relative);}
 let span=f32(visibility_tile_span_of(u32(view.size.x),u32(view.size.y)));let size=vec2<u32>(ceil(view.size/span));let cell=vec2<u32>(at/span);let head=finite_tiles[1u+cell.y*size.x+cell.x];
 if(head.w!=0u||head.z!=head.x){return finite_physical(pixel,depth,relative);}
 for(var i=0u;i<head.x;i++){
  let offset=head.y+i*2u;let nearest=bitcast<f32>(finite_tiles[(offset+1u)/4u][(offset+1u)%4u]);if(nearest<depth-relative||(!FINITE_GRID&&nearest<=depth+relative)){continue;}
  let primitive=finite_tiles[offset/4u][offset%4u];if(primitive==0u||primitive>arrayLength(&finite_projected)){continue;}
  let triangle=finite_projected[primitive-1u];let hit=projected_triangle_at(triangle,at);let tolerance=relative+0.00390625*(abs(triangle.gradient.x)+abs(triangle.gradient.y));
  if(hit.y>0.5&&!(clip_active()&&clip_cut_ndc(0u,clip_ndc(at,view.size,1.0-hit.x)))&&select(hit.x>depth+tolerance,hit.x>=depth-tolerance,FINITE_GRID)){return false;}
 }
 return true;
}


fn finite_alpha(pixel:vec2<f32>,axis:vec4<f32>,depths:vec2<f32>,flags:u32)->f32{
 let delta=axis.zw-axis.xy;let t=clamp(dot(pixel-axis.xy,delta)/max(dot(delta,delta),1e-20),0.,1.);let at=axis.xy+t*delta;let depth=mix(depths.x,depths.y,t);
 if(clip_active()&&clip_cut_ink_ndc(flags,clip_ndc(at,view.size,1.-depth))){return 0.;}
 let hidden=!finite_visible(pixel,axis,depths,flags);let glass=view.opacity>0.&&view.opacity<1.;return select(1.,select(0.,1.-view.opacity,glass),hidden&&view.opacity>0.);
}

fn finite_disc_alpha(pixel:vec2<f32>,centre:vec2<f32>,depth:f32,flags:u32)->f32{
 if(clip_active()&&clip_cut_ink_ndc(flags,clip_ndc(centre,view.size,1.-depth))){return 0.;}
 let tolerance=abs(depth)*DEPTH_REL_TOL+abs(1.-depth)*2.3841858e-7;
 let hidden=!finite_section(centre,depth,tolerance)||!ink_disc_visible(pixel,centre,depth,0u);
 let glass=view.opacity>0.&&view.opacity<1.;
 return select(1.,select(0.,1.-view.opacity,glass),hidden&&view.opacity>0.);
}

fn finite_visible(pixel:vec2<f32>,ends:vec4<f32>,depths:vec2<f32>,flags:u32)->bool{
 if(FINITE_GRID){return finite_grid_visible(pixel,ends,depths,flags);}
 let axis=finite_ink_axis(pixel,ends,depths);let tolerance=abs(axis.depth)*DEPTH_REL_TOL+abs(1.-axis.depth)*2.3841858e-7;
 if(!finite_section(axis.at,axis.depth,tolerance)){return false;}
 if(clip_active()&&clip_cut_ink_ndc(flags,clip_ndc(axis.at,view.size,1.-axis.depth))){return false;}
 var fit_at=pixel;let source_at=floor(axis.at)+fract(pixel);
 let at=finite_depth_pixel(pixel);let size=vec2<i32>(textureDimensions(finite_sections));
 if(all(at>=vec2<i32>(0))&&all(at<size)&&textureLoad(finite_sections,at,0).y>=0.5){fit_at=source_at;}
 let plane_visible=ink_visible_plane(fit_at,axis,0u);
 if(finite_tiles[0].x==0u){return plane_visible;}
 if(finite_source_hidden(fit_at,axis)){return false;}
 if(plane_visible){return true;}
 if(any(axis.at<vec2<f32>(0.))||any(axis.at>=view.size)){return false;}
 let span=f32(visibility_tile_span_of(u32(view.size.x),u32(view.size.y)));let size_px=vec2<u32>(ceil(view.size/span));let cell=vec2<u32>(axis.at/span);let head=finite_tiles[1u+cell.y*size_px.x+cell.x];
 if(head.w!=0u||head.z!=head.x){return false;}
 for(var i=0u;i<head.x;i++){
  let offset=head.y+i*2u;let nearest=bitcast<f32>(finite_tiles[(offset+1u)/4u][(offset+1u)%4u]);
  if(nearest<=axis.depth+abs(axis.depth)*DEPTH_REL_TOL){continue;}
  let primitive=finite_tiles[offset/4u][offset%4u];if(primitive==0u||primitive>arrayLength(&finite_projected)){continue;}
  let bounds=finite_projected[primitive-1u].bounds;
  if(any(axis.at<bounds.xy-vec2<f32>(SLOPE_PX))||any(axis.at>bounds.zw+vec2<f32>(SLOPE_PX))){continue;}
  if(finite_triangle_hides(primitive,axis)){return false;}
 }
 return true;
}
```

<a id="checkpoint-96zz-separate-hidden-ink-color-from-pick-ownership"></a>

### Step 19 of 21 · Separate hidden ink color from pick ownership (`96zz-separate-hidden-ink-color-from-pick-ownership`)

- **Goal:** Reveal hidden ink through glass without selecting an occluded original.
- **Data Flow Trace:** `Physical visibility → color attenuation or integer discard → original owner pick`
- **Files You Touch:** `src/finite_visibility.wgsl` (*Pass an explicit pick mode and reuse the shared hidden-ink alpha rule.*) · `src/finite_stroke.wgsl` (*Choose color attenuation or integer discard at the fragment call.*) · `src/finite_stroke_pick.wgsl` (*Choose color attenuation or integer discard at the fragment call.*) · `src/finite_chain.wgsl` (*Choose color attenuation or integer discard at the fragment call.*) · `src/finite_chain_pick.wgsl` (*Choose color attenuation or integer discard at the fragment call.*) · `src/finite_marker.wgsl` (*Choose color attenuation or integer discard at the fragment call.*) · `src/finite_marker_pick.wgsl` (*Choose color attenuation or integer discard at the fragment call.*) · `src/finite_visibility_tests.rs` (*Supply actual color, pre-color and post-color integer ownership checks for all three original ink families at both densities.*) · `src/opacity_gpu_tests.rs` (*Update the supplied glass fixture to require the current nearer face owner; retain exact blending, X-ray, restoration and reuse checks.*)

Translucency changes color, but it must not give hidden ink ownership of a pick.

The preceding predicates decide physical visibility. Reuse one alpha helper for strokes and points: color may attenuate hidden ink by one minus face opacity; integer picking discards it. Pass the mode explicitly from each color and pick fragment. Keep clipping as an unconditional discard. This removes the duplicated glass-alpha expression while keeping the fitted and exact visibility rules unchanged.

> **Self-Check Question:** *Why is a nonzero color alpha insufficient for picking?*  
> **Answer:** Hidden ink can be intentionally drawn through a translucent face. Its color contribution does not make its source physically visible.

- **What You Should See:** Hidden original ink can show through translucent faces. It cannot win an integer pick; the nearer original face keeps ownership.
- **Hands-On Lab Experiment:** Place a point, line or headed line behind a translucent face. Confirm that the ink is visible through it while a pick still selects the nearer original face. Repeat before drawing a color frame.

```wgsl
fn finite_hidden_alpha(hidden:bool,picking:bool)->f32{
 if(picking){return select(1.,0.,hidden);}
 let glass=view.opacity>0.&&view.opacity<1.;
 return select(1.,select(0.,1.-view.opacity,glass),hidden&&view.opacity>0.);
}
```

```wgsl
fn finite_alpha(pixel:vec2<f32>,axis:vec4<f32>,depths:vec2<f32>,flags:u32)->f32{
 let delta=axis.zw-axis.xy;let t=clamp(dot(pixel-axis.xy,delta)/max(dot(delta,delta),1e-20),0.,1.);let at=axis.xy+t*delta;let depth=mix(depths.x,depths.y,t);
 if(clip_active()&&clip_cut_ink_ndc(flags,clip_ndc(at,view.size,1.-depth))){return 0.;}
 let hidden=!finite_visible(pixel,axis,depths,flags);let glass=view.opacity>0.&&view.opacity<1.;return select(1.,select(0.,1.-view.opacity,glass),hidden&&view.opacity>0.);
}

 let glass=view.opacity>0.&&view.opacity<1.;
 return select(1.,select(0.,1.-view.opacity,glass),hidden&&view.opacity>0.);
}
```

```wgsl
// The tutorial still converts forward depth; allow two float rounding units until the reversed camera connects.
const FINITE_GRID:bool=false;
@group(1) @binding(0) var<storage,read> finite_projected:array<ProjectedTriangle>;
@group(1) @binding(1) var<storage,read> finite_tiles:array<vec4<u32>>;
@group(1) @binding(2) var finite_depth:texture_depth_2d;
@group(1) @binding(3) var finite_sections:texture_2d<f32>;
@group(1) @binding(4) var finite_primitive:texture_2d<u32>;
#include "ink_plane.wgsl"
fn finite_section(pixel:vec2<f32>,depth:f32,tolerance:f32)->bool{
 let at=finite_depth_pixel(pixel);let size=vec2<i32>(textureDimensions(finite_sections));if(any(at<vec2<i32>(0))||any(at>=size)){return true;}let cap=textureLoad(finite_sections,at,0).xy;if(cap.y<0.5){return true;}let plane=(u32(cap.y)-1u)&7u;let z=1.0-clip_plane_depth(plane,clip_ndc(pixel,view.size,0.0).xy);return select(z<=depth+tolerance,z<depth-tolerance,FINITE_GRID);
}
fn finite_physical(pixel:vec2<f32>,depth:f32,tolerance:f32)->bool{let at=finite_depth_pixel(pixel);let size=vec2<i32>(textureDimensions(finite_depth));if(any(at<vec2<i32>(0))||any(at>=size)){return false;}let z=1.0-textureLoad(finite_depth,at,0);return select(z<=depth+tolerance,z<depth-tolerance,FINITE_GRID);}
fn finite_screen(p:vec4<f32>)->vec2<f32>{return(p.xy/p.w*vec2<f32>(0.5,-0.5)+0.5)*view.size;}
fn finite_axis(a:vec4<f32>,b:vec4<f32>)->vec4<f32>{return vec4<f32>(finite_screen(a),finite_screen(b));}
fn finite_grid_visible(pixel:vec2<f32>,axis:vec4<f32>,depths:vec2<f32>,flags:u32)->bool{
 let delta=axis.zw-axis.xy;let length2=dot(delta,delta);let t=clamp(dot(pixel-axis.xy,delta)/max(length2,1e-20),0.0,1.0);let at=axis.xy+t*delta;let depth=mix(depths.x,depths.y,t);let relative=abs(depth)*1.9073486e-6+abs(1.0-depth)*2.3841858e-7;
 if(!finite_section(at,depth,relative)){return false;}
 if(clip_active()&&clip_cut_ink_ndc(flags,clip_ndc(at,view.size,1.0-depth))){return false;}
 if(any(at<vec2<f32>(0.0))||any(at>=view.size)){return finite_physical(pixel,depth,relative);}
 if(finite_tiles[0].x==0u){return finite_physical(pixel,depth,relative);}
 let span=f32(visibility_tile_span_of(u32(view.size.x),u32(view.size.y)));let size=vec2<u32>(ceil(view.size/span));let cell=vec2<u32>(at/span);let head=finite_tiles[1u+cell.y*size.x+cell.x];
 if(head.w!=0u||head.z!=head.x){return finite_physical(pixel,depth,relative);}
 for(var i=0u;i<head.x;i++){
  let offset=head.y+i*2u;let nearest=bitcast<f32>(finite_tiles[(offset+1u)/4u][(offset+1u)%4u]);if(nearest<depth-relative||(!FINITE_GRID&&nearest<=depth+relative)){continue;}
  let primitive=finite_tiles[offset/4u][offset%4u];if(primitive==0u||primitive>arrayLength(&finite_projected)){continue;}
  let triangle=finite_projected[primitive-1u];let hit=projected_triangle_at(triangle,at);let tolerance=relative+0.00390625*(abs(triangle.gradient.x)+abs(triangle.gradient.y));
  if(hit.y>0.5&&!(clip_active()&&clip_cut_ndc(0u,clip_ndc(at,view.size,1.0-hit.x)))&&select(hit.x>depth+tolerance,hit.x>=depth-tolerance,FINITE_GRID)){return false;}
 }
 return true;
}


fn finite_alpha(pixel:vec2<f32>,axis:vec4<f32>,depths:vec2<f32>,flags:u32,picking:bool)->f32{
 let delta=axis.zw-axis.xy;let t=clamp(dot(pixel-axis.xy,delta)/max(dot(delta,delta),1e-20),0.,1.);let at=axis.xy+t*delta;let depth=mix(depths.x,depths.y,t);
 if(clip_active()&&clip_cut_ink_ndc(flags,clip_ndc(at,view.size,1.-depth))){return 0.;}
 let hidden=!finite_visible(pixel,axis,depths,flags);return finite_hidden_alpha(hidden,picking);
}

fn finite_disc_alpha(pixel:vec2<f32>,centre:vec2<f32>,depth:f32,flags:u32,picking:bool)->f32{
 if(clip_active()&&clip_cut_ink_ndc(flags,clip_ndc(centre,view.size,1.-depth))){return 0.;}
 let tolerance=abs(depth)*DEPTH_REL_TOL+abs(1.-depth)*2.3841858e-7;
 let hidden=!finite_section(centre,depth,tolerance)||!ink_disc_visible(pixel,centre,depth,0u);
 return finite_hidden_alpha(hidden,picking);
}

fn finite_visible(pixel:vec2<f32>,ends:vec4<f32>,depths:vec2<f32>,flags:u32)->bool{
 if(FINITE_GRID){return finite_grid_visible(pixel,ends,depths,flags);}
 let axis=finite_ink_axis(pixel,ends,depths);let tolerance=abs(axis.depth)*DEPTH_REL_TOL+abs(1.-axis.depth)*2.3841858e-7;
 if(!finite_section(axis.at,axis.depth,tolerance)){return false;}
 if(clip_active()&&clip_cut_ink_ndc(flags,clip_ndc(axis.at,view.size,1.-axis.depth))){return false;}
 var fit_at=pixel;let source_at=floor(axis.at)+fract(pixel);
 let at=finite_depth_pixel(pixel);let size=vec2<i32>(textureDimensions(finite_sections));
 if(all(at>=vec2<i32>(0))&&all(at<size)&&textureLoad(finite_sections,at,0).y>=0.5){fit_at=source_at;}
 let plane_visible=ink_visible_plane(fit_at,axis,0u);
 if(finite_tiles[0].x==0u){return plane_visible;}
 if(finite_source_hidden(fit_at,axis)){return false;}
 if(plane_visible){return true;}
 if(any(axis.at<vec2<f32>(0.))||any(axis.at>=view.size)){return false;}
 let span=f32(visibility_tile_span_of(u32(view.size.x),u32(view.size.y)));let size_px=vec2<u32>(ceil(view.size/span));let cell=vec2<u32>(axis.at/span);let head=finite_tiles[1u+cell.y*size_px.x+cell.x];
 if(head.w!=0u||head.z!=head.x){return false;}
 for(var i=0u;i<head.x;i++){
  let offset=head.y+i*2u;let nearest=bitcast<f32>(finite_tiles[(offset+1u)/4u][(offset+1u)%4u]);
  if(nearest<=axis.depth+abs(axis.depth)*DEPTH_REL_TOL){continue;}
  let primitive=finite_tiles[offset/4u][offset%4u];if(primitive==0u||primitive>arrayLength(&finite_projected)){continue;}
  let bounds=finite_projected[primitive-1u].bounds;
  if(any(axis.at<bounds.xy-vec2<f32>(SLOPE_PX))||any(axis.at>bounds.zw+vec2<f32>(SLOPE_PX))){continue;}
  if(finite_triangle_hides(primitive,axis)){return false;}
 }
 return true;
}

fn finite_hidden_alpha(hidden:bool,picking:bool)->f32{
 if(picking){return select(1.,0.,hidden);}
 let glass=view.opacity>0.&&view.opacity<1.;
 return select(1.,select(0.,1.-view.opacity,glass),hidden&&view.opacity>0.);
}
```

<a id="checkpoint-96zza-connect-section-primitive-images"></a>

### Step 20 of 21 · Connect section primitives to color and first picking (`96zza-connect-section-primitive-images`)

- **Goal:** Share lossless cap addresses with mesh surfaces in color and first-pick images.
- **Data Flow Trace:** `Reserved plane address → shared integer output → matching color or cropped pick image → fitted ink`
- **Files You Touch:** `src/physical.wgsl` (*Own one lossless primitive encoder in the shared physical shader.*) · `src/clipping_gpu.rs` (*Assemble physical declarations once alongside clipping in every relevant shader.*) · `src/triangle.wgsl` (*Reuse the shared encoder instead of repeating the bit packing.*) · `src/triangle_pick.wgsl` (*Reuse the shared encoder instead of repeating the bit packing.*) · `src/ink_plane.wgsl` (*Decode a cap before any mesh lookup; reject unavailable projected addresses without clamping them to a different triangle.*) · `src/cap_color.wgsl` (*Add the integer output while keeping existing section consumers working.*) · `src/cap_pipeline.rs` (*Reuse the shared unblended integer target.*) · `src/caps_gpu.rs` (*Accept the renderer’s existing primitive view.*) · `src/renderer.rs` (*Pass the matching physical primitive image into the section pass.*) · `src/cap_pick.wgsl` (*Keep the original owner and section record beside the primitive output.*) · `src/cap_pick_pipeline.rs` (*Reuse the same integer target format without blending.*) · `src/cap_pick_gpu.rs` (*Attach the existing bounded crop image, loading previous mesh addresses.*) · `src/global_outline_tests.rs` (*Supply actual integer color, selection, clearing and pre-color cropped ownership checks at both physical display scales.*)

Connect a section cap to the same physical primitive images used by mesh surfaces.

Decode the reserved address before a projected-triangle lookup and use the clipping plane’s slope. Its low bit records selection; the rest identifies the plane. Negate the slope for the existing one-minus-forward depth adapter. Add the shared unblended RG16Uint target to both existing cap passes. Reuse one physical_primitive encoder for mesh and cap color/pick fragments. Write the marker’s low and high halves without float conversion, and load earlier mesh addresses so only covered cap pixels change. The color marker records selection; the first-pick marker is even, as in the current viewer. Keep original ownership and the separate float section output for existing masks and source tests. The supplied fixtures read both integer images at physical DPR1/2, including selected caps, empty pixels and picks before any color draw.

> **Self-Check Question:** *Why must a cap address bypass projected mesh storage?*  
> **Answer:** It describes a clipping plane, rather than a mesh triangle. Its plane gives the surface gradient, while the original owner still comes from the existing cap-picking records.

- **What You Should See:** Color and cropped first picks retain exact section addresses, matching depth and original ownership.
- **Hands-On Lab Experiment:** Cut and select a closed solid, then pick the section. Verify selected color, original ownership, and release. The supplied GPU checks additionally inspect the exact integer address before the first color frame.

```wgsl
struct CapFrame{size:vec2<f32>,origin:vec2<f32>}
@group(0) @binding(0) var<uniform> cap_frame:CapFrame;
@group(2) @binding(0) var counts:texture_2d<f32>;
const SAMPLES:u32=1u;const SELECT_COLOR:vec3<f32>=vec3<f32>(1.0,1.0,0.0);
fn count_at(at:vec2<i32>,sample:u32)->f32{return textureLoad(counts,at,0).x;}
// Prefixed in Rust: `counts` and `primitives` at group 3, SAMPLES, `count_at` and `primitive_at`.

// One corner of a fullscreen triangle; the instance picks the plane or the caps.
struct CapVertex {
    @builtin(position) pos: vec4<f32>, // clip position
    @location(0) @interpolate(flat) plane: u32, // clipping plane; for the masks 1 = selected caps only
}

@vertex
// A triangle over the whole target.
fn vs_cap(@builtin(vertex_index) vertex: u32, @builtin(instance_index) plane: u32) -> CapVertex {
    let xy = vec2<f32>(f32((vertex << 1u) & 2u), f32(vertex & 2u));
    var out: CapVertex;
    out.pos = vec4<f32>(xy * 2.0 - 1.0, 0.0, 1.0);
    out.plane = plane;
    return out;
}

// A section cap fragment: color, triangle id, the plane's depth and the samples it covers.
struct CapOut {
    @location(0) color: vec4<f32>, // hatch or light grey, with a black boundary
    @location(1) section_depth: vec2<f32>, // visible section depth and presence
    @location(2) primitive: vec2<u32>,
    @builtin(frag_depth) depth: f32, // depth of the plane here
    @builtin(sample_mask) mask: u32, // samples inside a solid and kept by the other planes
}

// Fraction of a pixel square lying below signed distance `t` from a line; the ribbon filter.
fn box_cdf(t: f32, hi: f32, lo: f32, m: f32, q: f32) -> f32 {
    let s = clamp(t, -hi, hi);
    let e = hi - abs(s);
    let tail = select(e * e / q, 1.0 - e * e / q, s > 0.0);
    return select(tail, 0.5 + s / m, abs(s) <= lo);
}

// Exact area of this pixel within `hw` of a line `d` away, across direction `g`.
fn band_area(d: f32, hw: f32, g: vec2<f32>) -> f32 {
    let a = abs(g.x);
    let b = abs(g.y);
    let hi = 0.5 * (a + b);
    let lo = 0.5 * abs(a - b);
    let m = max(max(a, b), 1e-6);
    let q = max(2.0 * a * b, 1e-6);
    return box_cdf(hw - d, hi, lo, m, q) + box_cdf(hw + d, hi, lo, m, q) - 1.0;
}


// Coverage of the nearest hatch line; `slope` is the change of `u` per pixel.
fn hatch_ink(u: f32, slope: vec2<f32>) -> f32 {
    let rate = max(length(slope), 1e-30); // scene units per pixel across the lines
    let level = log2(clipping.spacing * rate);
    // lines every power of two in scene units; every other one fades out over the last half octave
    let step = exp2(floor(level));
    let fade = smoothstep(0.5, 1.0, fract(level));
    let n = u / step;
    let nearest = round(n);
    let distance = abs(n - nearest) * step / rate;
    let odd = abs(nearest - 2.0 * round(nearest * 0.5)) > 0.5;
    let ink = clamp(band_area(distance, clipping.width * 0.5, slope / rate), 0.0, 1.0);
    return ink * select(1.0, 1.0 - fade, odd);
}

// Coverage of the section's union, including holes, without a border at the viewport edge.
fn section_coverage(at: vec2<i32>) -> f32 {
    if (any(at < vec2<i32>(0)) || any(at >= vec2<i32>(textureDimensions(counts)))) {
        return 1.0;
    }
    var covered = 0.0;
    for (var k = 0u; k < SAMPLES; k++) {
        covered += select(0.0, 1.0, count_at(at, k) > 0.5);
    }
    return covered / f32(SAMPLES);
}

fn section_outline(at: vec2<f32>) -> f32 {
    let directions = array<vec2<f32>, 8>(
        vec2<f32>(1.0, 0.0), vec2<f32>(-1.0, 0.0),
        vec2<f32>(0.0, 1.0), vec2<f32>(0.0, -1.0),
        vec2<f32>(0.7071, 0.7071), vec2<f32>(-0.7071, 0.7071),
        vec2<f32>(0.7071, -0.7071), vec2<f32>(-0.7071, -0.7071)
    );
    var edge = 0.0;
    for (var k = 0u; k < 8u; k++) {
        edge = max(edge, 1.0 - section_coverage(vec2<i32>(floor(at + directions[k] * clipping.outline))));
    }
    return edge;
}


@fragment
// Where the view ray meets a plane inside a closed solid: outlined hatch or solid grey.
fn fs_cap(in: CapVertex) -> CapOut {
    let i = in.plane;
    let ndc = clip_ndc(in.pos.xy + cap_frame.origin, cap_frame.size, 0.0).xy;
    let z = clip_plane_depth(i, ndc);
    let h = vec3<f32>(ndc, 1.0);
    // scene units across the hatch lines, and their change per pixel
    let u = dot(clipping.hatch[i].xyz, h) / dot(clipping.hatch_w[i].xyz, h);
    let slope = vec2<f32>(dpdx(u), dpdy(u));
    var kept = 0xffffffffu;
    var edge = 0.0;

    // the other planes cut the cap sample by sample
    for (var j = 0u; j < clipping.count; j++) {
        let t = dot(clipping.screen[j], vec4<f32>(ndc, z, 1.0));
        let gradient = vec2<f32>(dpdx(t), dpdy(t));
        let samples = clip_samples(t, gradient);
        kept &= select(samples, 0xffffffffu, j == i);
        if (j != i && dot(gradient, gradient) > 1e-30) {
            let distance = t / max(length(gradient), 1e-20);
            edge = max(edge, 1.0 - smoothstep(clipping.outline - 0.5, clipping.outline + 0.5, distance));
        }
    }

    let at = vec2<i32>(in.pos.xy);
    var inside = 0u;
    var selected = false;

    // inside a solid where more crossings leave than enter behind the plane
    for (var k = 0u; k < SAMPLES; k++) {
        let count = count_at(at, k);
        inside |= select(0u, 1u << k, count > 0.5);
        selected = selected || count > SELECTED_CROSSING - 0.5;
    }

    let mask = inside & kept;

    if (mask == 0u || !(z >= 0.0 && z <= 1.0)) {
        discard;
    }

    let paper = select(vec3<f32>(1.0), SELECT_COLOR, selected);
    let hatched = mix(paper, vec3<f32>(0.0), hatch_ink(u, slope));
    let solid = select(vec3<f32>(0.6), SELECT_COLOR, selected);
    edge = max(edge, section_outline(in.pos.xy));
    let color = mix(select(hatched, solid, clipping.fill == 1u), vec3<f32>(0.0), edge);
    let marker = CAP_PRIMITIVE + i * 2u + select(0u, 1u, selected);
    return CapOut(vec4<f32>(color, 1.0), vec2<f32>(z,f32(i+1u)+select(0.0,8.0,selected)), physical_primitive(marker), z, mask);
}
```
